      "content": "Output:\n```\n{\n \"app_name\": \"phone\",\n \"api_name\": \"search_contacts\",\n \"path\": \"/contacts\",\n \"method\": \"GET\",\n \"description\": \"Search your contact book for relatives' information.\",\n \"parameters\": [\n  {\n   \"name\": \"access_token\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"Access token obtained from phone app login.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"query\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Search query for the contacts list.\",\n   \"default\": \"\",\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"relationship\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"Relationship with the person in the contacts list to filter by.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_index\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The index of the page to return.\",\n   \"default\": 0,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 0.0\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"page_limit\",\n   \"type\": \"integer\",\n   \"required\": false,\n   \"description\": \"The maximum number of results to return per page.\",\n   \"default\": 5,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"value >= 1.0, <= 20.0\"\n   ]\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": [\n   {\n    \"contact_id\": 1,\n    \"first_name\": \"string\",\n    \"last_name\": \"string\",\n    \"email\": \"user@example.com\",\n    \"phone_number\": \"string\",\n    \"relationships\": [\n     \"string\"\n    ],\n    \"birthday\": \"string\",\n    \"home_address\": \"string\",\n    \"work_address\": \"string\",\n    \"created_at\": \"2019-01-01T00:00:00\"\n   }\n  ],\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",
      "role": "user"
    },
    {
      "content": "I need to search for contacts with the \"roommate\" relationship. Let me use the `search_contacts` API with the `relationship` parameter set to \"roommate\".\n\nCode:\n```python\nroommates = apis.phone.search_contacts(access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'], relationship='roommate')\nprint(roommates)\n```",
      "role": "assistant"
    },
    {
      "content": "Output:\n```\n[\n {\n  \"contact_id\": 748,\n  \"first_name\": \"Anthony\",\n  \"last_name\": \"Harrison\",\n  \"email\": \"an-harrison@gmail.com\",\n  \"phone_number\": \"2466064078\",\n  \"relationships\": [\n   \"roommate\"\n  ],\n  \"birthday\": \"1990-06-09\",\n  \"home_address\": \"247 Salinas Pines Suite 668\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n11799\",\n  \"work_address\": \"96967 Fox Loop Suite 397\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n86832\",\n  \"created_at\": \"2022-11-26T13:04:16\"\n },\n {\n  \"contact_id\": 749,\n  \"first_name\": \"Melissa\",\n  \"last_name\": \"Bailey\",\n  \"email\": \"mel.bailey@gmail.com\",\n  \"phone_number\": \"3383946795\",\n  \"relationships\": [\n   \"roommate\"\n  ],\n  \"birthday\": \"1986-08-02\",\n  \"home_address\": \"247 Salinas Pines Suite 668\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n11799\",\n  \"work_address\": \"774 Samuel Cape Suite 202\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n16844\",\n  \"created_at\": \"2022-11-26T13:04:16\"\n },\n {\n  \"contact_id\": 750,\n  \"first_name\": \"Nicholas\",\n  \"last_name\": \"Weber\",\n  \"email\": \"nicholas.weber@gmail.com\",\n  \"phone_number\": \"9503658964\",\n  \"relationships\": [\n   \"roommate\"\n  ],\n  \"birthday\": \"1986-10-19\",\n  \"home_address\": \"247 Salinas Pines Suite 668\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n11799\",\n  \"work_address\": \"716 White Drive Suite 52\\nSeattle\\nWashington\\nUnited States\\n47529\",\n  \"created_at\": \"2022-11-26T13:04:16\"\n }\n]\n```",

    {
      "content": "I have found three roommates: Anthony Harrison, Melissa Bailey, and Nicholas Weber. Now I need to send a voice message to each of them with the message \"I have taken out the trash.\"\n\nLet me check the `send_voice_message` API to see how to send a voice message.\n\nCode:\n```python\nprint(apis.api_docs.show_api_doc(app_name='phone', api_name='send_voice_message'))\n```",
      "role": "assistant"
    },
    {
      "content": "Output:\n```\n{\n \"app_name\": \"phone\",\n \"api_name\": \"send_voice_message\",\n \"path\": \"/messages/voice/{phone_number}\",\n \"method\": \"POST\",\n \"description\": \"Send a voice message on the given phone number.\",\n \"parameters\": [\n  {\n   \"name\": \"phone_number\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"The phone number of the contact to send the voice message to.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"message\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"The message text of the voice_message.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"length >= 1\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"access_token\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"Access token obtained from phone app login.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\",\n   \"voice_message_id\": 1\n  },\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",
      "role": "user"
    },
    {
      "content": "I need to send a voice message to each roommate using their phone number. Let me iterate over the roommates and send the message.\n\nCode:\n```python\nmessage = \"I have taken out the trash.\"\nfor roommate in roommates:\n    phone_number = roommate['phone_number']\n    print(f\"Sending voice message to {phone_number} with message: {message}\")\n    response = apis.phone.send_voice_message(phone_number=phone_number, message=message, access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'])\n    print(response)\n```",
      "role": "assistant"
    },
    {
      "content": "Output:\n```\nSending voice message to 2466064078 with message: I have taken out the trash.\n{\"message\": \"Voice message sent.\", \"voice_message_id\": 17021}\nSending voice message to 3383946795 with message: I have taken out the trash.\n{\"message\": \"Voice message sent.\", \"voice_message_id\": 17023}\nSending voice message to 9503658964 with message: I have taken out the trash.\n{\"message\": \"Voice message sent.\", \"voice_message_id\": 17025}\n```",
      "role": "user"

Let me call the `search_contacts` API with the relationship parameter set to \"roommate\".\n\nCode:\n```python\nroommate_contacts = apis.phone.search_contacts(access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'], relationship=\"roommate\")\nprint(roommate_contacts)\n```",

      "content": "I found three roommates: Anthony Harrison, Melissa Bailey, and Nicholas Weber. Now I need to send a voice message to each of them.     {
      "content": "Output:\n```\n{\n \"app_name\": \"phone\",\n \"api_name\": \"send_voice_message\",\n \"path\": \"/messages/voice/{phone_number}\",\n \"method\": \"POST\",\n \"description\": \"Send a voice message on the given phone number.\",\n \"parameters\": [\n  {\n   \"name\": \"phone_number\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"The phone number of the contact to send the voice message to.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"message\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"The message text of the voice_message.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": [\n    \"length >= 1\"\n   ]\n  },\n  {\n   \"name\": \"access_token\",\n   \"type\": \"string\",\n   \"required\": true,\n   \"description\": \"Access token obtained from phone app login.\",\n   \"default\": null,\n   \"constraints\": []\n  }\n ],\n \"response_schemas\": {\n  \"success\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\",\n   \"voice_message_id\": 1\n  },\n  \"failure\": {\n   \"message\": \"string\"\n  }\n }\n}\n```",
      "role": "user"

    {
      "content": "I can see that I need the phone number and message text to send a voice message. Let me prepare the message text and iterate through the roommate contacts to send the message.\n\nCode:\n```python\nmessage_text = \"I have taken out the trash.\"\nfor contact in roommate_contacts:\n    phone_number = contact['phone_number']\n    print(f\"Sending voice message to {contact['first_name']} {contact['last_name']} at {phone_number}\")\n    response = apis.phone.send_voice_message(phone_number=phone_number, message=message_text, access_token=<REDACTED:CREDENTIAL:SHA256=f542cec32c125ef1c0417e3dfcceb11c888f2a9f70c2c74265864412c0b7e859>'access_token'])\n    print(response)\n```",

## Interpretation Boundary

- **VERIFIED:** recorded prompts, outputs, executed code, observations, task outcomes, and first textual divergences.
- **INFERENCE:** a BEST-C/BEST-S difference is consistent with a memory-specific whole-bank effect.
- **UNVERIFIED:** causal mechanisms after the first logged divergence unless directly established by the trace.
